# Threads engagement log (@nolanpigi)

Tech-leader engagement passes for PING Social brand building (Nolan's 2026-09-15 directive: run engagement passes on tech leaders on Threads, including @mentions).

## Header rules (read before every pass)

- Targets: tech leaders, founders, builders, and technology voices. Seed list (verified on Threads 2026-09-15): @garyvee, @karpathy, @mcuban, @ycombinator. Discovery each pass may add more; verify the handle is really the person (verified badge or unmistakable identity) before engaging.
- NEVER re-engage a handled handle or post. The guard script enforces this mechanically: `~/workspace/outreach/engagement-guard.py` (platform `threads`) consults this file's durable history. Prose is not the enforcement.
- Caps per pass (Nolan's 2026-09-15 faster-ramp directive): up to 45 likes + 12 comments, every 2h. Pacing: 60 to 120 seconds between actions. Never exceed caps. One browser task per pass on this account; parallelism across platforms only.
- Reply voice: under 25 words, smart and specific, funny when it fits. React to something concrete in the post. @mention the author naturally when replying. Never sell, never mention PING Social, no links, no contact info, no em dashes.
- Stop instantly on any action block, checkpoint, verification challenge, rate-limit warning, or sign of reduced reach. Halve next pass's caps and surface it to Nolan immediately.
- Stay-on rule: this log's passes stay scheduled unless there is a real blocker or genuine flagged-account risk. A logged-out session is NOT a reason to stop the schedule: end that pass with zero engagement, log it, keep going next pass. Disable only on an actual restriction/action block/checkpoint, or Nolan saying stop.
- Quiet runs: only surface to Nolan on block, checkpoint, login failure, guard fail-closed, or a verify VIOLATION. Otherwise report only pass counts.

### Correction note, 2026-09-18 19:10 MDT (manual diagnostic, Nolan's prompt)

- Passes 35 and 36 were PROBE FALSE NEGATIVES, not session failures. The Threads/Instagram session in the Pig's ig-profile Chrome was alive the whole time (Nolan was right).
- Root cause: DOM drift in th-discover.js's login gate. Threads now renders profile hrefs WITHOUT a trailing slash (/@nolanpigi), but the gate's profileLinks regex required one (/^\/@[a-z0-9._]+\/$/). A genuinely logged-in home page therefore scored profileLinks=0 and createBtn=false, failing the gate.
- Evidence: read-only CDP diagnostics showed sessionid+ds_user_id cookies for both instagram.com and threads.com, open tabs rendering the logged-in "Home • Threads" feed with zero "Log in" prompts. Replicating the gate on a fresh page confirmed loginPrompt=0, profileLinks=0 (regex), createBtn=false on a page signed in as @nolanpigi.
- Fix: relaxed the profile-href regex to optional trailing slash in both the login gate and the signed_in_as detector in ~/workspace/pig-fb/th-discover.js. Re-ran the read-only probe: ok=true, logged_in=true, signed_in_as=@nolanpigi, 1 candidate from @garyvee.
- No browser restart, no login, no engagement actions were taken during this diagnostic. The "Nolan must log into Instagram" manual step from passes 35/36 is withdrawn.
- Capability record note: th-capability-state.json still marks threads-browser-automation BLOCKED/BROWSER_WEDGE (stale; wedge cleared at pass 35). Only ig-recovery-test.sh may flip BLOCKED->AVAILABLE; not run (it performs an IG canary like, unrequested). Next scheduled pass should succeed on the fixed probe and the record can be reconciled then.

### Investigation 2026-09-19 ~13:00 MDT (post-pass-44 engineering probe, Pigi) — CORRECTS the pass-44 hypothesis

- Pass 44's log entry above hypothesized "like-ACTION selector drift". Read-only live-session probes on the Pig FALSIFIED that: the failure is at the NAVIGATION boundary, not the selector.
- Observed (CDP :9226, headed ig-profile Chrome, read-only probes, zero clicks): `GET https://www.threads.com/@<handle>/post/<code>/` returns HTTP **302** (server-issued) to `https://www.threads.com/?injected_media_ids=["<media_id>"]`, which the SPA then normalizes to `https://www.threads.com/`. Reproduced 12/12 across 3 post URLs (@garyvee, @mosseri), with/without trailing slash, first and repeat goto in the same tab. navigator.webdriver=false.
- Consequence: the worker's thLike `page.goto(postUrl)` never lands on a post page, so `[aria-label="Like"]` legitimately matches 0 elements on the home feed. The 6x "like button not found" notes are the CORRECT fail-closed outcome for a missing post page, not a broken selector. The old selector was never evaluated against a post page in pass 44.
- Stage-by-stage DOM census (15s): no `[role="dialog"]`, no `<article>` elements, no post modal; the target post's text IS visible in the feed ("Post garyvee 1d I think incredibly long..."), and the only like-ish aria-label on the home page is one non-visible svg "Liked". No unambiguous like affordance for the target post exists in the reachable DOM.
- DECISION: NO selector patch applied. Patching `[aria-label="Like"]` would be guessing at the wrong layer; the defect is Threads server-side permalink handling for this session (rollout vs session-specific NOT determined). Per the stop rule, investigation halted rather than ship a speculative fix.
- Reclassification note for the next pass author: pass 44 is more precisely a navigation/dependency failure at the permalink boundary (server 302) than SURFACE_DRIFT of the like selector. The login-gate fix (09:15) remains proven and untouched; ig-engage.js was NOT modified in this investigation (no code change, nothing deployed).
- Probe scripts were run from Pig-local temp copies and removed afterwards; no Pig state left behind.
- Next-pass prognosis stands (likes will keep failing) but for a different reason: until post permalinks render a post page again, thLike cannot reach any like affordance. Candidate remedies (NOT attempted, need Nolan's call): like-from-feed flow redesign, or waiting out what may be a Threads-side rollout.

### Redesign 2026-09-19 ~13:05-13:45 MDT (Nolan-authorized): like-from-feed + seed follows

- Directive (Nolan, ~13:05 MDT): authorize the like-from-feed redesign; also follow the right accounts so feed likes land on the right type of accounts.
- Root cause (proven ~13:00): Threads post permalinks return server-issued HTTP 302 to home for this session (12/12 reproductions). The old thLike page.goto(permalink) never reached a post page. /following/ and author profile pages load normally (no 302).
- Follows (one-time, exactly the 6 authorized): @garyvee, @karpathy, @mcuban, @ycombinator, @mosseri, @zuck. First attempt found no <button> Follow control and stopped all 6 with zero clicks (correct fail-closed). Read-only probe then showed the profile Follow control is a div[role="button"] with exact text "Follow". Second attempt with the corrected selector: all 6 clicked at 60-120s pacing; read-only re-check shows Following state on all 6 profiles. No checkpoint, restriction, or block signals. 6/6 verified.
- Feed structure (read-only probes, Pig CDP :9226): /following/ renders post cards as DIVs (no <article>); each card holds exactly one permalink anchor /@handle/post/<code>/. Like control is div[role="button"] with NO aria-label/text; the svg inside carries <title>Like</title> (unliked) or <title>Unlike</title> (liked). NOTE: liked state is "Unlike", not "Liked" — first canary's verification looked for "Liked" and false-failed after a landed click; fixed before re-certification.
- thLike redesign (~/workspace/pig-fb/ig-engage.js, deployed to C:/Users/nolan/.local/share/pigi/ig-engage/ig-engage.js, sha256 60b808027da0ff28d47c062eee30bd5ab180555e7e7311d404772eb97790eb37, backup kept as .bak-20260919): parse handle+shortcode from the action URL; goto /following/; bounded 12-round scroll budget; locate the exact post by shortcode match in a permalink anchor; walk up to the largest ancestor holding exactly one permalink; author cross-check (container must link the expected handle); find the Like/Unlike control inside that container only; already-liked early return; trusted mouse click; verify Unlike state. Fail-closed notes: unparseable URL, post not in feed within budget, author mismatch (refuse), no like control. thComment and the login gate untouched. node --check clean.
- Re-certification: (1) read-only probe located /@garyvee/post/DdeSyPJkRk4 in the live Following feed with found/authorOk/likeControl all true, title "Like"; (2) canary attempt 1 on that post: click LANDED (post later showed Unlike state, like count visible) but worker reported failure because verification expected title "Liked" — verification bug, not a click bug; (3) after the Unlike-state fix + redeploy, canary attempt 2 on /@garyvee/post/DdczcHgARMq (read-only pre-check: title "Like", unliked): RESULT ok=true, canary_verified=true, note "like registered (Unlike state visible)". Exact unliked-to-liked transition proven end to end through the deployed worker.
